   692	describe("the declaration GATE: a declaration that is not closed never becomes a surface", () => {
   693	  // A minimal CLOSED surface. Every guard fixture below is this surface
   694	  // with exactly ONE reference broken, and each pair proves discrimination:
   695	  // the broken form throws with its own problem, the corrected form does
   696	  // not throw at all.
   697	  const closed = (over: Partial<SurfaceDecl> = {}, rootOver: Record<string, unknown> = {}): SurfaceDecl =>
   698	    ({
   699	      substrate: templateFormat.substrate,
   700	      valueClasses: {
   701	        idClass: { kind: "string", tag: "vc", rows: ["x"], grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "bad" } },
   702	      },
   703	      crossRules: [],
   704	      normalizers: [],
   705	      root: {
   706	        kind: "map.fixed",
   707	        tag: "root",
   708	        rows: ["x"],
   709	        containerMessage: "c",
   710	        unknownMessage: "u",
   711	        fields: { id: { kind: "valueClass", tag: "id", rows: ["x"], valueClass: "idClass" } },
   712	        ...rootOver,
   713	      },
   714	      ...over,
   715	    });
   716	
   717	  interface Guard {
   718	    readonly claim: string;
   719	    /** The surface with ONE reference broken. */
   720	    readonly broken: SurfaceDecl;
   721	    /** The problem the gate must name. */
   722	    readonly problem: RegExp;
   723	  }
   724	
   725	  const node = (extra: Record<string, unknown>): SurfaceDecl =>
   726	    closed({}, { fields: { id: { kind: "valueClass", tag: "id", rows: ["x"], valueClass: "idClass", ...extra } } });
   727	
   728	  const withSelector = (path: string): SurfaceDecl =>
   729	    closed({}, {
   730	      fields: {
   731	        names: { kind: "map.open", tag: "n", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container", entry: { kind: "string", tag: "ne", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" } },
   732	        pick: { kind: "string", tag: "p", rows: ["x"], memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: path }, message: "m" } },
   733	      },
   734	    });
   735	
   736	  const GUARDS: readonly Guard[] = [
   737	    {
   738	      claim: "an unknown value-class name — the measured shame case",
   739	      broken: closed({}, { fields: { id: { kind: "valueClass", tag: "id", rows: ["x"], valueClass: "TYPO" } } }),
   740	      problem: /value class "TYPO" is not declared/u,
   741	    },
   742	    {
   743	      claim: "a selector path in the documented-but-inert `..` form",
   744	      broken: withSelector("..names"),
   745	      problem: /selector path "\.\.names" must start with "\$"/u,
   746	    },
   747	    {
   748	      claim: "a selector path with an empty segment",
   749	      broken: withSelector("$..names"),
   750	      problem: /has an empty segment/u,
   751	    },
   752	    {
   753	      claim: "a selector path with `*` inside a segment",
   754	      broken: withSelector("$.na*mes"),
   755	      problem: /"\*" is legal only as a whole segment/u,
   756	    },
   757	    {
   758	      claim: "a channel mark where the engine does not read one",
   759	      broken: closed({}, {
   760	        fields: {
   761	          list: { kind: "list", tag: "l", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", memberLaneAt: "index",
   762	            member: { kind: "string", tag: "lm", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t", channel: "file" } },
   763	        },
   764	      }),
   765	      problem: /carries channel "file", which is read only on a field of a map\.fixed/u,
   766	    },
   767	    {
   768	      claim: "a dependsOn naming a tag nothing declares",
   769	      broken: closed({}, {
   770	        fields: {
   771	          uses: { kind: "string", tag: "u", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" },
   772	          config: { kind: "delegate", tag: "c", rows: ["x"], registry: "gateCatalog", by: "uses",
   773	            beltMessage: "b", dependsOn: ["no-such-tag"] },
   774	        },
   775	      }),
   776	      problem: /dependsOn names "no-such-tag", which is not a declared tag/u,
   777	    },
   778	    {
   779	      claim: "a tag declared twice",
   780	      broken: node({ tag: "root" }),
   781	      problem: /tag "root" is declared more than once/u,
   782	    },
   783	    {
   784	      claim: "a node citing no ratified row",
   785	      broken: node({ rows: [] }),
   786	      problem: /cites no ratified row/u,
   787	    },
   788	    {
   789	      claim: "an issue code outside the closed namespace",
   790	      broken: closed({}, {
   791	        fields: { id: { kind: "enum", tag: "id", rows: ["x"], members: [{ value: "a" }], message: "m", code: "made_up_code" } },
   792	      }),
   793	      problem: /issue code "made_up_code" is outside the declared namespace/u,
   794	    },
   795	    {
   796	      claim: "a message using a placeholder the engine does not supply",
   797	      broken: closed({}, { unknownMessage: "unknown {notASlot}" }),
   798	      problem: /unknown placeholder \{notASlot\}/u,
   799	    },
   800	    {
   801	      claim: "a finding-path template written in selector syntax",
   802	      broken: closed({
   803	        crossRules: [{
   804	          tag: "eq", rows: ["x"], relation: "equals",
   805	          left: { keysOf: "$.id" }, right: { keysOf: "$.id" },
   806	          missingFromLeft: { at: "$.roles", message: "m" },
   807	          missingFromRight: { at: "roles", message: "m" },
   808	        }],
   809	      }),
   810	      problem: /uses selector syntax; a finding path is rendered, not walked/u,
   811	    },
   812	    {
   813	      claim: "a normalizer operand path that is not document-rooted",
   814	      broken: closed({
   815	        normalizers: [{ tag: "n", rows: ["x"], hook: "expandAdvancesRound", over: "steps", edges: "t", advanceSet: "$.r", into: "f" }],
   816	      }),
   817	      problem: /normalizer path "steps" must start with "\$"/u,
   818	    },
   819	    {
   820	      claim: "a grammar that is not a valid regular expression",
   821	      broken: closed({
   822	        valueClasses: { idClass: { kind: "string", tag: "vc", rows: ["x"], grammar: { re: "^[a-z", message: "bad" } } },
   823	      }),
   824	      problem: /is not a valid regular expression/u,
   825	    },
   826	  ];
   827	
   828	  for (const guard of GUARDS) {
   829	    it(`${guard.claim} — the gate REFUSES it`, () => {
   830	      expect(() => defineSurface(guard.broken)).toThrow(SurfaceDeclarationError);
   831	      expect(closureProblems(guard.broken).join("\n")).toMatch(guard.problem);
   832	    });
   833	  }
   834	
   835	  it("DISCRIMINATION: the same surface with every reference resolved passes the gate", () => {
   836	    // Every fixture above is `closed()` with ONE thing broken. If the gate
   837	    // fired unconditionally, this would throw too — which is what makes the
   838	    // refusals above evidence rather than noise.
   839	    expect(closureProblems(closed())).toStrictEqual([]);
   840	    expect(() => defineSurface(closed())).not.toThrow();
   841	    expect(closureProblems(withSelector("$.names"))).toStrictEqual([]);
   842	  });
   843	
   844	  it("the guard register is complete, unique and PINNED", () => {
   845	    expect(GUARDS).toHaveLength(13);
   846	    expect(new Set(GUARDS.map((guard) => guard.claim)).size).toBe(GUARDS.length);
   847	  });
   848	
   849	  it("the LIVE declaration is closed — no unresolved reference reaches a document", () => {
   850	    expect(closureProblems(templateFormat)).toStrictEqual([]);
   851	  });
   852	
   853	  it("the LIVE declaration is frozen at EVERY level, not only the root (ADR-019 D4)", () => {
   854	    const root = templateFormat.root as unknown as { readonly fields: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>> };
   855	    const nested = root.fields["start"]?.["memberOf"] as { message: string };

codex
Az első teljes összevetés két erős jelöltet mutat: a selectoroknál a kapu jelenleg csak a nyelvtant ellenőrzi, nem azt, hogy a deklarált csomópont valóban létezik; továbbá a `delegate.by` és a normalizer mezőnevei dinamikus feloldások, de nem látszik hozzájuk lezárási ellenőrzés. Most ezeket futtatott minimálpéldákkal erősítem meg, és külön ellenőrzöm a value-class belsejében lévő `dependsOn` bejárását.

   191	/** Interpolate a declared message template. The slot set is CLOSED and
   192	 * supplied by the engine from the node's own evaluation context — no slot
   193	 * carries rule-specific knowledge. */
   194	export function renderMessage(template: MessageTemplate, slots: Slots): string {
   195	  return render(template, slots);
   196	}
   197	
   198	function render(template: MessageTemplate, slots: Slots): string {
   199	  return template.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/gu, (whole, name: string): string => {
   200	    switch (name) {
   201	      case "path":
   202	        return slots.path ?? whole;
   203	      case "key":
   204	        return slots.key === undefined ? whole : describeKey(slots.key);
   205	      case "keyJson":
   206	        return JSON.stringify(slots.key) ?? whole;
   207	      case "value":
   208	        return "value" in slots ? describeValue(slots.value) : whole;
   209	      case "valueRaw":
   210	        return "value" in slots ? String(slots.value) : whole;
   211	      case "valueJson":
   212	        return "value" in slots ? (JSON.stringify(slots.value) ?? String(slots.value)) : whole;
   213	      case "source":
   214	        return slots.source ?? whole;
   215	      case "grammar":
   216	        return slots.grammar ?? whole;
   217	      case "members":
   218	        return slots.members ?? whole;
   219	      case "keys":
   220	        return slots.keys ?? whole;
   221	      case "label":
   222	        return slots.label ?? whole;
   223	      case "ownerKey":
   224	        return slots.ownerKey ?? whole;
   225	      default:
   226	        return whole;
   227	    }
   228	  });
   229	}
   230	
   231	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   232	// The run state.
   233	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   234	
   235	interface Frame {
   236	  /** The declaration path of the node being evaluated (`$.steps.*.role`). */
   237	  readonly decl: DeclPath;
   238	  /** The value path the findings address (`steps.review.role`). */
   239	  readonly path: string;
   240	  readonly value: unknown;
   241	  /** The enclosing container's value — the operand of a `..` selector. */
   242	  readonly parentValue: unknown;
   243	  /** The key of the nearest enclosing OPEN-map entry. */
   244	  readonly ownerKey?: string | undefined;
   245	  readonly label?: string | undefined;
   246	}
   247	
   248	/** What a node evaluation reports upward: whether the node produced a
   249	 * value its dependents may rely on (the implicit container precondition
   250	 * plus every DECLARED `gating`). */
   251	interface NodeResult {
   252	  readonly ok: boolean;
   253	  readonly value?: unknown;
   254	}
   255	
   256	/** A membership lane whose operand has NOT been evaluated yet. The engine
   257	 * defers it to the end of the walk rather than reading an operand whose
   258	 * reliability is still unknown — a GENERAL property of the `memberOf`
   259	 * construct, not a per-rule placement. */
   260	interface DeferredCheck {
   261	  readonly frame: Frame;
   262	  readonly rule: MembershipRule;
   263	  readonly candidate: unknown;
   264	  readonly slots: Slots;
   265	}
   266	
   267	class Run {
   268	  readonly findings: ValidationFinding[] = [];
   269	  readonly effectiveConfigs = new Map<string, unknown>();
   270	  readonly runtimeContextBindings: string[] = [];
   271	  /** Declaration path / tag → whether every instance produced a usable
   272	   * value. A rule naming an unreliable operand is suppressed. */
   273	  readonly declOk = new Map<string, boolean>();
   274	  /** Declaration paths whose evaluation has completed. An operand that is
   275	   * neither reliable nor unreliable but PENDING defers its rule. */
   276	  readonly completed = new Set<DeclPath>();
   277	  readonly deferred: DeferredCheck[] = [];
   278	  readonly memo = new WeakMap<object, unknown>();
   279	  root: unknown;
   280	
   281	  constructor(
   282	    readonly channel: EngineChannel,
   283	    readonly registries: Readonly<Record<string, GateCatalog | undefined>>,
   284	    readonly valueClasses: Readonly<Record<string, NodeDecl>>,
   285	  ) {}
   286	
   287	  /** The injected set a selector root or a delegation names. */
   288	  registry(name: string): GateCatalog | undefined {
   289	    return this.registries[name];
   290	  }
   291	
   292	  emit(path: string, message: string, code?: string): void {
   293	    this.findings.push(code === undefined ? { path, message } : { path, message, code });
   294	  }
   295	
   296	  mark(key: string, ok: boolean): void {
   297	    if (this.declOk.get(key) === false) return;
   298	    this.declOk.set(key, ok);
   299	  }
   300	
   301	  reliable(key: string): boolean {
   302	    return this.declOk.get(key) !== false;
   303	  }
   304	}
   305	
   306	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   307	// Selectors.
   308	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   309	
   310	type SelectorResult =
   311	  | { readonly status: "ok"; readonly values: readonly string[] }
   312	  | { readonly status: "unreliable" | "pending" };
   313	
   314	const UNRELIABLE = { status: "unreliable" } as const;
   315	const PENDING = { status: "pending" } as const;
   316	
   317	/** Walk a declaration path over the value graph, expanding `*` over open
   318	 * maps. `..` resolves one level up from the citing frame. A prefix that
   319	 * has not been evaluated yet answers PENDING, not "unreliable". */
   320	function resolvePath(
   321	  run: Run,
   322	  from: Frame,
   323	  path: DeclPath,
   324	): { readonly status: "ok"; readonly nodes: unknown[] } | { readonly status: "unreliable" | "pending" } {
   325	  const segments = path.split(".");
   326	  let nodes: unknown[];
   327	  let declPrefix: string;
   328	  const head = segments.shift();
   329	  if (head === "$") {
   330	    nodes = [run.root];
   331	    declPrefix = "$";
   332	  } else if (head === "^") {
   333	    // Parent-relative: the citing node's own container.
   334	    nodes = [from.parentValue];
   335	    declPrefix = from.decl.split(".").slice(0, -1).join(".");
   336	  } else {
   337	    return UNRELIABLE;
   338	  }
   339	  for (const segment of segments) {
   340	    declPrefix = `${declPrefix}.${segment}`;
   341	    if (!run.reliable(declPrefix)) return UNRELIABLE;
   342	    if (!run.completed.has(declPrefix)) return PENDING;
   343	    const next: unknown[] = [];
   344	    for (const node of nodes) {
   345	      if (!isContainer(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   346	      if (segment === "*") {
   347	        for (const [key, child] of entriesOf(node)) {
   348	          if (typeof key === "string") next.push(child);
   349	        }
   350	      } else {
   351	        if (!containerHas(node, segment)) return UNRELIABLE;
   352	        next.push(containerGet(node, segment));
   353	      }
   354	    }
   355	    nodes = next;
   356	  }
   357	  return { status: "ok", nodes };
   358	}
   359	
   360	function evaluateSelector(run: Run, from: Frame, selector: Selector): SelectorResult {
   361	  if ("injected" in selector) {
   362	    // D8's ADMITTED widening: the selector root is an INJECTED set. A
   363	    // registry resolves rather than enumerates, so membership is asked of
   364	    // it directly (`isMember`); the set itself is never listed.
   365	    return run.registry(selector.injected) === undefined ? UNRELIABLE : { status: "ok", values: [] };
   366	  }
   367	  if ("union" in selector) {
   368	    const parts = selector.union.map((part) => evaluateSelector(run, from, part));
   369	    if (parts.some((part) => part.status === "pending")) return PENDING;
   370	    if (parts.some((part) => part.status === "unreliable")) return UNRELIABLE;
   371	    return {
   372	      status: "ok",
   373	      values: parts.flatMap((part) => (part.status === "ok" ? part.values : [])),
   374	    };
   375	  }
   376	  const target = "keysOf" in selector ? selector.keysOf : "valuesOf" in selector ? selector.valuesOf : selector.collect;
   377	  const resolved = resolvePath(run, from, target);
   378	  if (resolved.status !== "ok") return resolved;
   379	  const values: string[] = [];
   380	  for (const node of resolved.nodes) {
   381	    if ("keysOf" in selector) {
   382	      if (!isContainer(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   383	      for (const [key] of entriesOf(node)) if (typeof key === "string") values.push(key);
   384	    } else if ("valuesOf" in selector) {
   385	      if (!Array.isArray(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   386	      for (const item of node) if (typeof item === "string") values.push(item);
   387	    } else {
   388	      if (typeof node !== "string") return UNRELIABLE;
   389	      values.push(node);
   390	    }
   391	  }
   392	  return { status: "ok", values };
   393	}
   394	
   395	/** `memberOf` against an INJECTED root asks the registry; against a
   396	 * document root it asks the collected set. */
   397	function isMember(run: Run, selector: Selector, set: SelectorResult, candidate: string): boolean {
   398	  if ("injected" in selector) return run.registry(selector.injected)?.resolve(candidate) != null;
   399	  return set.status === "ok" && set.values.includes(candidate);
   400	}
   401	
   402	function checkMembership(
   403	  run: Run,
   404	  frame: Frame,
   405	  rule: MembershipRule,
   406	  candidate: unknown,
   407	  slots: Slots,
   408	  local?: ReadonlyMap<string, boolean>,
   409	): void {
   410	  if (rule.dependsOn?.some((tag) => local?.get(tag) === false || !run.reliable(tag)) === true) return;
   411	  const set = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.target);
   412	  if (set.status === "pending") {
   413	    run.deferred.push({ frame, rule, candidate, slots });
   414	    return;
   415	  }
   416	  if (set.status === "unreliable") return;
   417	  const at = rule.at === "container" ? parentPath(frame.path) : frame.path;
   418	  const hit = typeof candidate === "string" && isMember(run, rule.target, set, candidate);
   419	  const violated = rule.relation === "disjointFrom" ? hit : !hit;
   420	  if (violated) {
   421	    run.emit(at, render(rule.message, { ...slots, path: at, value: candidate }), rule.code);
   422	  }
   423	}
   424	
   425	function parentPath(path: string): string {
   426	  const bracket = path.lastIndexOf("[");
   427	  const dot = path.lastIndexOf(".");
   428	  if (bracket > dot) return path.slice(0, bracket);
   429	  if (dot < 0) return "$";
   430	  return path.slice(0, dot);
   700	  };
   701	}
   702	
   703	function evalMapOpen(
   704	  run: Run,
   705	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.open" }>,
   706	  frame: Frame,
   707	): NodeResult {
   708	  const value = frame.value;
   709	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   710	  if (!isContainer(value)) {
   711	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   712	    return { ok: false };
   713	  }
   714	  const container = value;
   715	  const entries = entriesOf(container);
   716	  let ok = true;
   717	  if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && entries.length === 0) {
   718	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots));
   719	    if (decl.nonempty.gating === true) ok = false;
   720	  }
   721	  if (decl.deepKeyStringness?.channel === run.channel.kind) {
   722	    scanKeyStringness(run, container, frame.path, decl.deepKeyStringness.message, new WeakSet());
   723	  }
   724	  const normalized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   725	  for (const [key, child] of entries) {
   726	    const keyIsString = typeof key === "string";
   727	    const entryPath = keyIsString ? joinPath(frame.path, key) : frame.path;
   728	    if (decl.keyClass !== undefined) {
   729	      const keyFrame: Frame = {
   730	        decl: `${frame.decl}<key>`,
   731	        path: decl.keyLaneAt === "container" ? frame.path : entryPath,
   732	        value: key,
   733	        parentValue: container,
   734	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   735	      };
   736	      const keyResult = evaluateNode(run, decl.keyClass, keyFrame);
   737	      if (!keyResult.ok && decl.keyClass.gating === true) ok = false;
   738	    }
   739	    if (decl.keysSubsetOf !== undefined) {
   740	      const before = run.findings.length;
   741	      // The rule is the OPEN MAP's, so a `^`-relative selector resolves
   742	      // from the map's own container — never from the map itself.
   743	      const subsetFrame: Frame = {
   744	        decl: frame.decl,
   745	        path: entryPath,
   746	        value: key,
   747	        parentValue: frame.parentValue,
   748	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   749	      };
   750	      checkMembership(run, subsetFrame, decl.keysSubsetOf, key, {
   751	        ...slots,
   752	        path: entryPath,
   753	        key,
   754	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   755	      });
   756	      // A key outside the subset is DEAD config: nothing below it has an
   757	      // operand to be validated against (the measured lane's CONTINUE).
   758	      if (run.findings.length > before) continue;
   759	    }
   760	    const entryFrame: Frame = {
   761	      decl: `${frame.decl}.*`,
   762	      path: entryPath,
   763	      value: child,
   764	      parentValue: container,
   765	      ownerKey: keyIsString ? key : frame.ownerKey,
   766	    };
   767	    const entryResult = evaluateNode(run, decl.entry, entryFrame);
   768	    if (keyIsString && entryResult.ok && entryResult.value !== undefined) {
   769	      defineOwn(normalized, key, entryResult.value);
   770	    }
   771	  }
   772	  run.completed.add(`${frame.decl}.*`);
   773	  return { ok, value: normalized };
   774	}
   775	
   776	/** The gates subtree's file-channel key-STRINGNESS scan: a non-string key
   777	 * cannot become an own property and would blind every own-key scan, so it
   778	 * reports at the NEAREST addressable path (the containing map's). */
   779	function scanKeyStringness(
   780	  run: Run,
   781	  value: unknown,
   782	  path: string,
   783	  message: MessageTemplate,
   784	  seen: WeakSet<object>,
   785	): void {
   786	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return;
   787	  if (seen.has(value)) return;
   788	  seen.add(value);
   789	  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   790	    value.forEach((item, index) => scanKeyStringness(run, item, indexPath(path, index), message, seen));
   791	    return;
   792	  }
   793	  if (!isResolvedMap(value)) return;
   794	  for (const [key, child] of value.entries()) {
   795	    if (typeof key !== "string") {
   796	      run.emit(path, render(message, { path, key, value: key }));
   797	      continue;
   798	    }
   799	    scanKeyStringness(run, child, joinPath(path, key), message, seen);
   800	  }
   801	}
   802	
   803	function evalList(
   804	  run: Run,
   805	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "list" }>,
   806	  frame: Frame,
   807	  local?: LocalOk,
   808	): NodeResult {
   809	  const value = frame.value;
   810	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   811	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
   812	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   813	    return { ok: false };
   814	  }
   815	  let ok = true;
   816	  if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && value.length === 0) {
   817	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots));
   818	    if (decl.nonempty.gating === true) ok = false;
   819	  }
   820	  const normalized: unknown[] = [];
   821	  const seen = new Set<unknown>();
   822	  const clean: { readonly member: unknown; readonly path: string }[] = [];
   823	  value.forEach((member, index) => {
   824	    const memberPath = decl.memberLaneAt === "container" ? frame.path : indexPath(frame.path, index);
   825	    const memberFrame: Frame = {
   826	      decl: `${frame.decl}[]`,
   827	      path: memberPath,
   828	      value: member,
   829	      parentValue: value,
   830	      ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   831	    };
   832	    const before = run.findings.length;
   833	    const result = evaluateNode(run, decl.member, memberFrame, local);
   834	    if (run.findings.length > before) return;
   835	    if (result.value !== undefined) normalized.push(result.value);
   836	    if (decl.memberOf !== undefined) {
   837	      checkMembership(run, memberFrame, decl.memberOf, member, { ...slots, path: memberPath, value: member });
   838	    }
   839	    if (decl.unique?.grain === "perOccurrence") {
   840	      const at = decl.unique.at === "container" ? frame.path : memberPath;
   841	      if (seen.has(member)) {
   842	        run.emit(at, render(decl.unique.message, { ...slots, path: at, value: member }));
   843	      }
   844	      seen.add(member);
   845	    }
   846	    clean.push({ member, path: memberPath });
   847	  });
   848	  if (decl.disjointFrom !== undefined) {
   849	    for (const entry of clean) {
   850	      const memberFrame: Frame = {
   851	        decl: `${frame.decl}[]`,
   852	        path: entry.path,
   853	        value: entry.member,
   854	        parentValue: value,
   855	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   856	      };
   857	      checkMembership(run, memberFrame, decl.disjointFrom, entry.member, {
   858	        ...slots,
   859	        path: entry.path,
   860	        value: entry.member,
   861	      });
   862	    }
   863	  }
   864	  run.completed.add(`${frame.decl}[]`);
   865	  return { ok, value: normalized };
   866	}
   867	
   868	function evalString(
   869	  run: Run,
   870	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "string" }>,
   871	  frame: Frame,
   872	  local?: LocalOk,
   873	): NodeResult {
   874	  const value = frame.value;
   875	  const slots: Slots = { ...baseSlots(frame), grammar: decl.grammar?.re };
   876	  if (typeof value !== "string") {
   877	    // A node that declares NO type lane leaves the non-string case to its
   878	    // membership lane, which emits ONE finding for both faults (the
   879	    // measured form of `start` and of a transition target).
   880	    if (decl.typeMessage !== undefined) {
   881	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.typeMessage, slots), decl.presence?.code);
   882	      return { ok: false };
   883	    }
   884	  } else {
   885	    if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && value.length === 0) {
   886	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots), decl.presence?.code);
   887	      return { ok: false };
   888	    }
   889	    if (decl.grammar !== undefined && !new RegExp(decl.grammar.re, "u").test(value)) {
   890	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.grammar.message, slots));
   891	      return { ok: false };
   892	    }
   893	  }
   894	  if (decl.memberOf !== undefined) {
   895	    const before = run.findings.length;
   896	    checkMembership(run, frame, decl.memberOf, value, slots, local);
   897	    if (run.findings.length > before) return { ok: false, value };
   898	  }
   899	  return typeof value === "string" ? { ok: true, value } : { ok: false };
   900	}
   901	
   902	function evalInteger(
   903	  run: Run,
   904	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "integer" }>,
   905	  frame: Frame,
   906	): NodeResult {
   907	  const value = frame.value;
   908	  if (decl.sourceForm === "plainDecimalInteger" && run.channel.kind === "file") {
   909	    const node: unknown = run.channel.doc.getIn(astPath(frame.path), true);
   910	    const message = sourceLadderFinding(node, run.channel.source, frame.path);
   911	    if (message !== undefined) {
   912	      run.emit(frame.path, message);
   913	      return { ok: false };
   914	    }
   915	    return { ok: true, value };
   916	  }
   917	  const belt = decl.resolvedForm;
   918	  if (belt?.safeInteger === true) {
   919	    if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < belt.min) {
   920	      run.emit(frame.path, render(belt.message, baseSlots(frame)), decl.presence?.code);
   921	      return { ok: false };
   922	    }
   923	  }
   924	  return { ok: true, value };
   925	}
   926	
   927	function evalEnum(
   928	  run: Run,
   929	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "enum" }>,
   930	  frame: Frame,
   931	): NodeResult {
   932	  const value = frame.value;
   933	  const legal = decl.members.filter(
   934	    (one) => one.channel === undefined || one.channel === "both" || one.channel === run.channel.kind,
   935	  );
   936	  const slots: Slots = {
   937	    ...baseSlots(frame),
   938	    members: legal.map((one) => String(one.value)).join(", "),
   939	  };
   940	  const member = legal.find((one) => one.value === value);
   941	  if (member !== undefined) return { ok: true, value: member.store ?? member.value };
   942	  run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.message, slots), decl.code);
   943	  return { ok: false };
   944	}
   945	
   946	function evalUnion(
   947	  run: Run,
   948	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "union" }>,
   949	  frame: Frame,
   950	): NodeResult {
   951	  const value = frame.value;
   952	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   953	  if (typeof value === "string") {
   954	    const removed = decl.removedValues?.[value];
   955	    if (removed !== undefined) {
   956	      run.emit(frame.path, render(removed, slots));
   957	      return { ok: false };
   958	    }
   959	    if (decl.literals?.includes(value) === true) return { ok: true, value };
   960	  }
   961	  if (isContainer(value) && decl.mapCase !== undefined) {
   962	    return evaluateNode(run, decl.mapCase, frame);
   963	  }
   964	  run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.message, slots));
   965	  return { ok: false };
   966	}
   967	
   968	function evalRaw(run: Run, decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "raw" }>, frame: Frame): NodeResult {
   969	  if (decl.containerMessage !== undefined && !isContainer(frame.value)) {
   970	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, baseSlots(frame)));
   971	    return { ok: false };
   972	  }
   973	  return { ok: true, value: materialize(frame.value, run.memo) };
   974	}
   975	
   976	function evalMapPlain(
   977	  run: Run,
   978	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.plain" }>,
   979	  frame: Frame,
   980	): NodeResult {
   981	  const value = frame.value;
   982	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   983	  const realized = materialize(value, run.memo);
   984	  if (!isPlainMap(realized)) {
   985	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, { ...slots, value: realized }));
   986	    return { ok: false };
   987	  }
   988	  if (!isCanonicalizable(realized)) {
   989	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.canonicalJsonSafe.message, slots));
   990	    return { ok: false };
   991	  }
   992	  return { ok: true, value: realized };
   993	}
   994	
   995	function evalDelegate(
   996	  run: Run,
   997	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "delegate" }>,
   998	  frame: Frame,
   999	  local?: LocalOk,
  1000	): NodeResult {
  1001	  if (decl.dependsOn?.some((tag) => local?.get(tag) === false) === true) return { ok: true };
  1002	  const owner = frame.parentValue;
  1003	  const by = isContainer(owner) ? containerGet(owner, decl.by) : undefined;
  1004	  if (typeof by !== "string") return { ok: true };
  1005	  const registration = run.registry(decl.registry)?.resolve(by) ?? null;
  1006	  if (registration === null) return { ok: true };
  1007	  if (registration.requiresRuntimeContext) run.runtimeContextBindings.push(parentPath(frame.path));
  1008	  const raw = frame.value === undefined ? undefined : materialize(frame.value, run.memo);
  1009	  const result = registration.validateAndNormalizeConfig(raw);
  1010	  if (result.ok) {
  1011	    run.effectiveConfigs.set(parentPath(frame.path), result.effective);
  1012	    return { ok: true, value: result.effective };
  1013	  }
  1014	  if (result.findings.length === 0) {
  1015	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.beltMessage, { ...baseSlots(frame), key: by, value: by }));
  1016	    return { ok: false };
  1017	  }
  1018	  for (const finding of result.findings) {
  1019	    const at = finding.path === "" ? frame.path : `${frame.path}.${finding.path}`;
  1020	    run.emit(at, finding.message, finding.code);
  1021	  }
  1022	  return { ok: false };
  1023	}
  1024	
  1025	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1026	// Cross rules and the deferred queue.
  1027	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1028	
  1029	function unique(values: readonly string[]): string[] {
  1030	  return [...new Set(values)];
  1031	}
  1032	
  1033	/** vocabulary #12's `equals` — two-direction set equality, each direction
  1034	 * carrying its own path grain and wording (ch8-C16's measured form). */
  1035	function evaluateEquals(run: Run, frame: Frame, rule: EqualsRuleDecl): void {
  1036	  if (rule.dependsOn?.some((tag) => !run.reliable(tag)) === true) return;
  1037	  const left = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.left);
  1038	  const right = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.right);
  1039	  if (left.status !== "ok" || right.status !== "ok") return;
  1040	  const declared = unique(left.values);
  1041	  const used = unique(right.values);
  1042	  for (const value of used) {
  1043	    if (declared.includes(value)) continue;
  1044	    const at = render(rule.missingFromLeft.at, { value });
  1045	    run.emit(at, render(rule.missingFromLeft.message, { path: at, value }));
  1046	  }
  1047	  for (const value of declared) {
  1048	    if (used.includes(value)) continue;
  1049	    const at = render(rule.missingFromRight.at, { value });
  1050	    run.emit(at, render(rule.missingFromRight.message, { path: at, value }));
  1051	  }
  1052	}
  1053	
  1054	function runDeferred(run: Run): void {
  1055	  // A membership lane deferred for a PENDING operand runs once, in the
  1056	  // order the walk queued it. The queue is drained, never re-fed: every
  1057	  // operand has completed by now, so a second pass could only loop.
  1058	  const queued = run.deferred.splice(0, run.deferred.length);
  1059	  for (const check of queued) {
  1060	    checkMembership(run, check.frame, check.rule, check.candidate, check.slots);
  1061	  }
  1062	  run.deferred.length = 0;
  1063	}
  1064	
  1065	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1066	// Entry points.
  1067	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1068	
  1069	/**
  1070	 * Run a surface declaration over a value graph. ONE computation serves
  1071	 * both channels (D1): the caller supplies `channel`, and the source-bearing
  1072	 * attributes are inert where no source exists.
  1073	 */
  1074	export function runSurface(surface: SurfaceDecl, value: unknown, opts: EngineOptions): EngineRun {
  1075	  const run = new Run(opts.channel, { gateCatalog: opts.catalog }, surface.valueClasses);
  1076	  run.root = value;
  1077	  const rootFrame: Frame = { decl: "$", path: "$", value, parentValue: undefined };
  1078	  const rootDecl = surface.root;
  1079	
  1080	  // The root container precondition: with no map operand nothing below has
  1081	  // an address, so the ONE finding is the whole result. It applies where
  1082	  // the ROOT is declared as a map — a surface whose root is any other
  1083	  // kind evaluates through its own node like every other.
  1084	  const rootIsMap =
  1085	    rootDecl.kind === "map.fixed" || rootDecl.kind === "map.open" || rootDecl.kind === "map.plain";
  1086	  if (rootIsMap && !isContainer(value)) {
  1087	    run.emit("$", render(rootDecl.containerMessage, { path: "$", value }));
  1088	    return {
  1089	      findings: run.findings,
  1090	      normalized: undefined,
  1091	      effectiveConfigs: run.effectiveConfigs,
  1092	      runtimeContextBindings: run.runtimeContextBindings,
  1093	    };
  1094	  }
  1095	
  1096	  // The substrate's `resolve.graph: acyclic` lane. It ACCUMULATES with the
  1097	  // structural lanes (a cycle is not a container, so it suppresses
  1098	  // nothing), and it has an operand only where a resolved graph exists.
  1099	  if (run.channel.kind === "file") {
  1100	    const cycle = findCycle(value, "$", new Set());
  1101	    if (cycle !== undefined) {
  1102	      run.emit(cycle, render(surface.substrate.resolve.graph.message, { path: cycle }));
  1103	    }
  1104	  }
  1105	
  1106	  const result = evaluateNode(run, rootDecl, rootFrame);
  1107	  runDeferred(run);
  1108	  for (const rule of surface.crossRules) {
  1109	    if (rule.relation === "equals") evaluateEquals(run, rootFrame, rule);
  1110	  }
  1111	  return {
  1112	    findings: run.findings,
  1113	    normalized: result.value,
  1114	    effectiveConfigs: run.effectiveConfigs,
  1115	    runtimeContextBindings: run.runtimeContextBindings,
  1116	  };
  1117	}
     1	import type { NormalizerHookDecl, SurfaceDecl } from "./vocabulary.js";
     2	
     3	/**
     4	 * ADR-019 D3: THE NORMALIZER — the engine's SECOND capability, named so
     5	 * that "schema" is never read as covering it.
     6	 *
     7	 * A declaration says what is LEGAL; it does not compute a value. Admission
     8	 * both validates and PRODUCES the admitted form (the audit's residual R4:
     9	 * `advancesRound` expanded per transition, the effective gate config
    10	 * materialized). Those are TRANSFORMS, and they live here.
    11	 *
    12	 * The boundary is exact and load-bearing:
    13	 * - plain `default:` materialization IS declarable and stays SCHEMA-side
    14	 *   (the engine applies it while walking — `activation`, `runtimeContext`,
    15	 *   `output.mode`, the disposition and reason defaults);
    16	 * - DERIVATION — a value computed from other values — is the normalizer's.
    17	 *
    18	 * Each hook is a NAMED capability parameterized by declared operand paths,
    19	 * so adding one is D7 format growth (a ratified amendment), never an edit
    20	 * that slips in under "normalization".
    21	 */
    22	
    23	/** Own-property WRITE: a step id or event type legally admits `__proto__`. */
    24	function defineOwn<T>(target: Record<string, T>, key: string, value: T): void {
    25	  Object.defineProperty(target, key, { configurable: true, enumerable: true, value, writable: true });
    26	}
    27	
    28	function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
    29	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    30	}
    31	
    32	function ownGet(record: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): unknown {
    33	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(record, key) ? record[key] : undefined;
    34	}
    35	
    36	/** Resolve a declared operand path (`$.steps`, `$.round.advanceOnArrivalAt`)
    37	 * over the normalized value. */
    38	function at(root: unknown, path: string): unknown {
    39	  let cursor: unknown = root;
    40	  for (const segment of path.split(".")) {
    41	    if (segment === "$") continue;
    42	    if (!isRecord(cursor)) return undefined;
    43	    cursor = ownGet(cursor, segment);
    44	  }
    45	  return cursor;
    46	}
    47	
    48	/**
    49	 * R4's first member (ch11-C39): expand each entry's edge map into a
    50	 * COMPLETE per-edge boolean map against the declared advancing set. An
    51	 * absent declaration yields all-false (ch11-C38's none-default); the
    52	 * PRODUCER MONOPOLY holds — the map is recomputed and overwrites whatever
    53	 * the input carried.
    54	 */
    55	function expandFlagMaps(
    56	  root: Record<string, unknown>,
    57	  hook: Extract<NormalizerHookDecl, { hook: "expandAdvancesRound" }>,
    58	): void {
    59	  const entries = at(root, hook.over);
    60	  if (!isRecord(entries)) return;
    61	  const declared = at(root, hook.advanceSet);
    62	  const advancing = new Set<string>(Array.isArray(declared) ? (declared as string[]) : []);
    63	  for (const key of Object.keys(entries)) {
    64	    const entry = ownGet(entries, key);
    65	    if (!isRecord(entry)) continue;
    66	    const edges = ownGet(entry, hook.edges);
    67	    const flags: Record<string, boolean> = {};
    68	    if (isRecord(edges)) {
    69	      for (const edge of Object.keys(edges)) {
    70	        defineOwn(flags, edge, advancing.has(ownGet(edges, edge) as string));
    71	      }
    72	    }
    73	    defineOwn(entry, hook.into, flags);
    74	  }
    75	}
    76	
    77	/**
    78	 * R4's second member (ch11-C20/C5): write each registration's EFFECTIVE
    79	 * config into the binding's single config surface. The binding is rebuilt
    80	 * from its CARRIED fields plus the effective config — the one downstream
    81	 * config form.
    82	 */
    83	function materializeConfigs(
    84	  root: Record<string, unknown>,
    85	  hook: Extract<NormalizerHookDecl, { hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs" }>,
    86	  effectiveConfigs: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>,
    87	): void {
    88	  // `over` addresses the pipeline map through two open-map levels:
    89	  // `$.steps.*.gates` — the entry key and the event key are the wildcards.
    90	  const [ownerPath, ...rest] = hook.over.split(".*.");
    91	  const owners = at(root, ownerPath ?? "$");
    92	  if (!isRecord(owners)) return;
    93	  const inner = rest.join(".*.");
    94	  for (const ownerKey of Object.keys(owners)) {
    95	    const owner = ownGet(owners, ownerKey);
    96	    if (!isRecord(owner)) continue;
    97	    const pipelines = ownGet(owner, inner);
    98	    if (!isRecord(pipelines)) continue;
    99	    const rebuiltPipelines: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   100	    for (const eventKey of Object.keys(pipelines)) {
   101	      const pipeline = ownGet(pipelines, eventKey);
   102	      if (!Array.isArray(pipeline)) continue;
   103	      const base = `${ownerPath === "$" ? "" : `${(ownerPath ?? "").replace("$.", "")}.`}${ownerKey}.${inner}.${eventKey}`;
   104	      defineOwn(
   105	        rebuiltPipelines,
   106	        eventKey,
   107	        pipeline.map((binding: unknown, index: number) => {
   108	          const carried: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   109	          if (isRecord(binding)) {
   110	            for (const field of hook.carry) defineOwn(carried, field, ownGet(binding, field));
   111	          }
   112	          defineOwn(carried, hook.into, effectiveConfigs.get(`${base}[${String(index)}]`));
   113	          return carried;
   114	        }),
   115	      );
   116	    }
   117	    defineOwn(owner, inner, rebuiltPipelines);
   118	  }
   119	}
   120	
   121	/**
   122	 * Run every declared normalizer hook over a VALIDATED value. The caller
   123	 * guarantees the value carries no findings — a declaration that refused
   124	 * the input never reaches derivation.
   125	 */
   126	export function normalize(
   127	  surface: SurfaceDecl,
   128	  value: unknown,
   129	  effectiveConfigs: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>,
   130	): unknown {
   131	  if (!isRecord(value)) return value;
   132	  for (const hook of surface.normalizers) {
   133	    if (hook.hook === "expandAdvancesRound") {
   134	      expandFlagMaps(value, hook);
   135	      continue;
   136	    }
   137	    materializeConfigs(value, hook, effectiveConfigs);
   138	  }
   139	  return value;
   140	}
     1	import type { GateCatalog } from "../../ports/index.js";
     2	import type { ValidationFinding } from "../errors.js";
     3	import type { EngineChannel } from "./engine.js";
     4	import { astPath, runSurface, sourceLadderFinding } from "./engine.js";
     5	import { normalize } from "./normalizer.js";
     6	import { templateFormat } from "./templateFormat.js";
     7	
     8	/**
     9	 * The template surface, composed: ONE engine run over the declaration on
    10	 * whichever channel the caller is on, plus the AUDITED RESIDUAL — the
    11	 * lanes that ADR-019 D1 leaves to prose/code — wired into the SAME finding
    12	 * pipeline so the output is one uniform stream.
    13	 *
    14	 * The residual realized here, by audit family:
    15	 *
    16	 * - **R3** the existential cross-rule over resolved registrations
    17	 *   (ch11-C19 → ch12-C5): "IF any binding resolves to a registration whose
    18	 *   `requiresRuntimeContext` is true THEN `$.runtimeContext` must be a
    19	 *   spec map" is a conditional over a DERIVED property of an injected
    20	 *   object; a `when:` general enough to express it is an expression
    21	 *   language.
    22	 * - **R7** the uses-scoped SOURCE ladder on two gate-config integers
    23	 *   (ch11-C12's source half). Its declaration exists — `[vc-authored-int]`
    24	 *   — but realizing it inside the engine requires the `delegate` hand-off
    25	 *   to carry the CHANNEL into the registration, which is a change to the
    26	 *   ratified `GateRegistration` port shape: out of ADR-019 D2's scope and
    27	 *   exactly the "while we are here" D6 forbids. Under D7's ≥2-row test the
    28	 *   alternative — a construct that scopes a source lane by a sibling's
    29	 *   value, serving this one row — is refused, and the row keeps a code
    30	 *   lane. This is R7's first live member; the family was kept open at the
    31	 *   ratification for precisely this case.
    32	 *
    33	 * Not here, and deliberately: **R5** (the store's declared-ref/filename
    34	 * check) stays the store stage's, and **R6** is library-owned wording.
    35	 */
    36	
    37	export interface SurfaceOutcome {
    38	  readonly findings: readonly ValidationFinding[];
    39	  /** The admitted VALUE, unbranded: ch11-P2a A6 keeps `admitTemplate` the
    40	   * ONLY sanctioned producer of the `AdmittedTemplate` brand, and that
    41	   * guard is lint-enforced. The engine computes the value; admission
    42	   * brands it. */
    43	  readonly admitted?: unknown;
    44	}
    45	
    46	const RUNTIME_CONTEXT_CODE = "runtime_context_required_for_process_gate";
    47	
    48	/** R7: the two uses-scoped integer fields whose SOURCE form is laddered by
    49	 * the walk. The scoping is SYNTACTIC — the authored `uses` string, no
    50	 * catalog resolution — exactly as the measured lane does it. */
    51	const SOURCE_SCOPED_CONFIG_FIELDS: Readonly<Record<string, readonly string[]>> = {
    52	  "declarative.threshold": ["value"],
    53	  "external.process": ["timeoutMs"],
    54	};
    55	
    56	function isContainer(value: unknown): value is ReadonlyMap<unknown, unknown> | Record<string, unknown> {
    57	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    58	}
    59	
    60	function entriesOf(
    61	  value: ReadonlyMap<unknown, unknown> | Record<string, unknown>,
    62	): readonly (readonly [unknown, unknown])[] {
    63	  if (value instanceof Map) return [...value.entries()];
    64	  return Object.keys(value).map((key) => [key, (value as Record<string, unknown>)[key]] as const);
    65	}
    66	
    67	function get(value: unknown, key: string): unknown {
    68	  if (!isContainer(value)) return undefined;
    69	  if (value instanceof Map) return value.get(key);
    70	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, key) ? (value as Record<string, unknown>)[key] : undefined;
    71	}
    72	
    73	/** R7's walk: `steps.<id>.gates.<event>[<i>].config.<field>` for the two
    74	 * scoped fields, on the file channel only (no source, no lane). */
    75	function sourceScopedFindings(value: unknown, channel: EngineChannel): ValidationFinding[] {
    76	  if (channel.kind !== "file") return [];
    77	  const findings: ValidationFinding[] = [];
    78	  const steps = get(value, "steps");
    79	  if (!isContainer(steps)) return findings;
    80	  for (const [stepId, step] of entriesOf(steps)) {
    81	    if (typeof stepId !== "string" || !isContainer(step)) continue;
    82	    const gates = get(step, "gates");
    83	    if (!isContainer(gates)) continue;
    84	    for (const [eventType, pipeline] of entriesOf(gates)) {
    85	      if (typeof eventType !== "string" || !Array.isArray(pipeline)) continue;
    86	      pipeline.forEach((binding: unknown, index: number) => {
    87	        if (!isContainer(binding)) return;
    88	        const uses = get(binding, "uses");
    89	        const config = get(binding, "config");
    90	        if (typeof uses !== "string" || !isContainer(config)) return;
    91	        for (const field of SOURCE_SCOPED_CONFIG_FIELDS[uses] ?? []) {
    92	          if (get(config, field) === undefined) continue;
    93	          const path = `steps.${stepId}.gates.${eventType}[${String(index)}].config.${field}`;
    94	          const node: unknown = channel.doc.getIn(astPath(path), true);
    95	          const message = sourceLadderFinding(node, channel.source, path);
    96	          if (message !== undefined) findings.push({ path, message });
    97	        }
    98	      });
    99	    }
   100	  }
   101	  return findings;
   102	}
   103	
   104	/**
   105	 * R3: exactly ONE template-grain finding when a resolved process gate
   106	 * demands a runtime context the template does not provision. The lane is
   107	 * a DEPENDENT of the `runtimeContext` node: an illegal value fired its own
   108	 * container finding and left the normalized field absent, which suppresses
   109	 * this one (ch8-C21's container-precondition rule).
   110	 */
   111	function runtimeContextCrossRule(
   112	  normalized: unknown,
   113	  bindings: readonly string[],
   114	): ValidationFinding | undefined {
   115	  if (bindings.length === 0) return undefined;
   116	  const requirement = get(normalized, "runtimeContext");
   117	  if (requirement === undefined || isContainer(requirement)) return undefined;
   118	  return {
   119	    path: "runtimeContext",
   120	    code: RUNTIME_CONTEXT_CODE,
   121	    message:
   122	      `template declares process gate(s) requiring runtime context but does not ` +
   123	      `declare a provisionable runtimeContext spec { kind, provider } (gates: ${bindings.join(", ")})`,
   124	  };
   125	}
   126	
   127	/**
   128	 * Run the template surface on ONE channel. All-or-nothing: any finding
   129	 * means no admitted value exists (ch8-C22), and the admitted form is
   130	 * produced by the NORMALIZER, never by the validator (D3).
   131	 */
   132	export function runTemplateSurface(
   133	  value: unknown,
   134	  channel: EngineChannel,
   135	  catalog: GateCatalog,
   136	): SurfaceOutcome {
   137	  const run = runSurface(templateFormat, value, { channel, catalog });
   138	  const findings: ValidationFinding[] = [...run.findings];
   139	  findings.push(...sourceScopedFindings(value, channel));
   140	  const crossRule = runtimeContextCrossRule(run.normalized, run.runtimeContextBindings);
   141	  if (crossRule !== undefined) findings.push(crossRule);
   142	  if (findings.length > 0) return { findings };
   143	  return { findings: [], admitted: normalize(templateFormat, run.normalized, run.effectiveConfigs) };
   144	}
   220	    ...resolvedDuplicateFindings(doc, lineCounter),
   221	  ].sort((a, b) => a.offset - b.offset);
   222	  for (const { finding } of positionedErrors) {
   223	    parseFindings.push(finding);
   224	  }
   225	  for (const warning of doc.warnings) {
   226	    parseFindings.push(parseFinding(warning));
   227	  }
   228	  if (parseFindings.length > 0) {
   229	    return fail("parse", parseFindings);
   230	  }
   231	
   232	  // RESOLVE stage (G8/draft C5+C36): toJS — the alias-amplification
   233	  // guard throws HERE, invisible to doc.errors (probes P8/P14/P16).
   234	  let value: unknown;
   235	  try {
   236	    // Preserve resolved map-key types through validation. The default
   237	    // object materialization would collapse distinct YAML keys such as
   238	    // `1` and `"1"` before V5 can reject the non-string key.
   239	    value = doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true });
   240	  } catch (error) {
   241	    return fail("resolve", [{ stage: "resolve", message: errorMessage(error) }]);
   242	  }
   243	
   244	  // VALIDATE stage: ONE run of the template surface's declared schema
   245	  // (ADR-019 D1) over the resolved value graph, on the FILE channel so
   246	  // the source-bearing lanes have an operand. The two rungs this stage
   247	  // used to have — the source-form walk and the admission rung — are one
   248	  // computation over one declaration; their findings were already
   249	  // required to accumulate in ONE `validate` result (ch11-P4 F7), and
   250	  // now they cannot fail to. The result stays XOR (ch8-C22): an admitted
   251	  // template escapes only when the run is findings-free.
   252	  try {
   253	    const admitted = admitFromSource(value, doc, text, opts.catalog ?? EMPTY_CATALOG);
   254	    if (!admitted.ok) {
   255	      return fail("validate", admitted.findings);
   256	    }
   257	    return { ok: true, template: admitted.template };
   258	  } catch (error) {
   259	    // The C22 every-stage belt: no known input reaches this (V15's
   260	    // cycle rule is a FINDING, not a throw), but the stage still maps.
   261	    return fail("validate", [
   262	      {
   263	        path: SUBSTRATE.internalFailure.path,
   264	        message: renderMessage(SUBSTRATE.internalFailure.message, { value: errorMessage(error) }),
   265	      },
   266	    ]);
   267	  }
   268	}

   520	    return undefined;

   525	
   526	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   527	// Node evaluation.
   528	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   529	
   530	const DEFAULT_MISSING = 'missing required key "{key}"';
   531	
   532	/** Sibling reliability inside ONE fixed-map evaluation. `dependsOn` reads
   533	 * it before the global map so a defect in binding #1 cannot suppress a
   534	 * lane in binding #2. */
   535	type LocalOk = Map<string, boolean>;
   536	
   537	function baseSlots(frame: Frame): Slots {
   538	  return { path: frame.path, value: frame.value, ownerKey: frame.ownerKey, label: frame.label };
   539	}
   540	
   541	function evaluateNode(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   542	  const result = dispatch(run, decl, frame, local);
   543	  run.completed.add(frame.decl);
   544	  run.mark(frame.decl, result.ok);
   545	  run.mark(decl.tag, result.ok);
   546	  return result;
   547	}
   548	
   549	function dispatch(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   550	  switch (decl.kind) {
   551	    case "map.fixed":
   552	      return evalMapFixed(run, decl, frame);
   553	    case "map.open":
   554	      return evalMapOpen(run, decl, frame);
   555	    case "map.plain":
   556	      return evalMapPlain(run, decl, frame);
   557	    case "list":
   558	      return evalList(run, decl, frame, local);
   559	    case "string":
   560	      return evalString(run, decl, frame, local);
   561	    case "integer":
   562	      return evalInteger(run, decl, frame);
   563	    case "enum":
   564	      return evalEnum(run, decl, frame);
   565	    case "union":
   566	      return evalUnion(run, decl, frame);
   567	    case "raw":
   568	      return evalRaw(run, decl, frame);
   569	    case "valueClass":
   570	      return evalValueClassRef(run, decl, frame, local);
   571	    case "delegate":
   572	      return evalDelegate(run, decl, frame, local);
   573	  }
   574	}
   575	
   576	function evalValueClassRef(
   577	  run: Run,
   578	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "valueClass" }>,
   579	  frame: Frame,
   580	  local?: LocalOk,
   581	): NodeResult {
   582	  const target = run.valueClasses[decl.valueClass];
   583	  if (target === undefined) return { ok: true, value: frame.value };
   584	  const merged: Frame = { ...frame, label: decl.label ?? frame.label };
   585	  const result = dispatch(run, target, merged, local);
   586	  // The REFERENCING site's gating decides, not the shared class's.
   587	  if (!result.ok && decl.gating === true) run.mark(frame.decl, false);
   588	  return result;
   589	}
   590	
   591	function evalMapFixed(
   592	  run: Run,
   593	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.fixed" }>,
   594	  frame: Frame,
   595	): NodeResult {
   596	  const value = frame.value;
   597	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   598	  if (!isContainer(value)) {
   599	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   600	    return { ok: false };
   601	  }
   602	  const container = value;
   603	  const fieldNames = Object.keys(decl.fields);
   604	  // A field declared for ONE channel is legal only there — the same
   605	  // channel scoping D1 applies to source-bearing attributes, applied to
   606	  // keyset membership.
   607	  const inChannel = (name: string): boolean => {
   608	    const channel = decl.fields[name]?.channel;
   609	    return channel === undefined || channel === "both" || channel === run.channel.kind;
   610	  };
   611	  // A channel-scoped field is scoped WHOLE: out of channel it is neither a
   612	  // legal key, nor a missing one, nor evaluated (design review F7).
   613	  const scoped = fieldNames.filter(inChannel);
   614	  const legal = new Set(scoped);
   615	  const normalized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   616	  const local: LocalOk = new Map();
   617	
   618	  const emitUnknownKeys = (): void => {
   619	    for (const [key] of entriesOf(container)) {
   620	      if (typeof key !== "string") {
   621	        run.emit(
   622	          frame.path,
   623	          render(decl.unknownMessage, { ...slots, key, value: key, keys: scoped.join(", ") }),
   624	        );
   625	        continue;
   626	      }
   627	      if (legal.has(key)) continue;
   628	      const at = joinPath(frame.path, key);
   629	      const removed = decl.removedKeys?.[key];
   630	      const keySlots: Slots = { ...slots, path: at, key, value: key, keys: scoped.join(", ") };
   631	      run.emit(at, render(removed ?? decl.unknownMessage, keySlots));
   632	    }
   633	  };
   634	
   635	  const emitMissing = (name: string): void => {
   636	    const field = decl.fields[name];
   637	    const presence = field?.presence;
   638	    if (field === undefined || presence?.required !== true) return;
   639	    if (containerHas(container, name)) return;
   640	    if (presence.foldedIntoTypeLane === true) {
   641	      // Absence is this node's OWN type lane (a binding's `uses`).
   642	      evaluateNode(run, field, childFrame(frame, name, undefined, container), local);
   643	      local.set(field.tag, false);
   644	      return;
   645	    }
   646	    const at = presence.at === "self" ? joinPath(frame.path, name) : frame.path;
   647	    run.emit(
   648	      at,
   649	      render(presence.message ?? decl.missingMessage ?? DEFAULT_MISSING, { ...slots, path: at, key: name }),
   650	      presence.code,
   651	    );
   652	    local.set(field.tag, false);
   653	  };
   654	
   655	  const evalOneField = (name: string): void => {
   656	    const field = decl.fields[name];
   657	    if (field === undefined) return;
   658	    if (!containerHas(container, name)) {
   659	      // An ABSENT field is where a declared `default:` materializes — the
   660	      // schema-side half of ADR-019 D3.
   661	      if (field.default !== undefined) defineOwn(normalized, name, field.default);
   662	      return;
   663	    }
   664	    const child = childFrame(frame, name, containerGet(container, name), container);
   665	    const result = evaluateNode(run, field, child, local);
   666	    local.set(field.tag, result.ok);
   667	    if (result.ok && result.value !== undefined) defineOwn(normalized, name, result.value);
   668	  };
   669	
   670	  const order = decl.laneOrder ?? "unknownThenPerKey";
   671	  if (order === "missingThenUnknown") {
   672	    for (const name of scoped) emitMissing(name);
   673	    emitUnknownKeys();
   674	    for (const name of scoped) evalOneField(name);
   675	  } else if (order === "unknownThenMissingThenValues") {
   676	    emitUnknownKeys();
   677	    for (const name of scoped) emitMissing(name);
   678	    for (const name of scoped) evalOneField(name);
   679	  } else {
   680	    emitUnknownKeys();
   681	    for (const name of scoped) {
   682	      if (!containerHas(container, name)) {
   683	        emitMissing(name);
   684	        evalOneField(name);
   685	        continue;
   686	      }
   687	      evalOneField(name);
   688	    }
   689	  }
   690	  return { ok: true, value: normalized };
   960	  }
   961	  if (isContainer(value) && decl.mapCase !== undefined) {
   962	    return evaluateNode(run, decl.mapCase, frame);
   963	  }
   964	  run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.message, slots));
   965	  return { ok: false };
   966	}
   967	
   968	function evalRaw(run: Run, decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "raw" }>, frame: Frame): NodeResult {
   969	  if (decl.containerMessage !== undefined && !isContainer(frame.value)) {
   970	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, baseSlots(frame)));
   971	    return { ok: false };
   972	  }
   973	  return { ok: true, value: materialize(frame.value, run.memo) };
   974	}
   975	
   976	function evalMapPlain(
   977	  run: Run,
   978	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.plain" }>,
   979	  frame: Frame,
   980	): NodeResult {
   981	  const value = frame.value;
   982	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   983	  const realized = materialize(value, run.memo);
   984	  if (!isPlainMap(realized)) {
   985	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, { ...slots, value: realized }));
   986	    return { ok: false };
   987	  }
   988	  if (!isCanonicalizable(realized)) {
   989	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.canonicalJsonSafe.message, slots));
   990	    return { ok: false };
   991	  }
   992	  return { ok: true, value: realized };
   993	}
   994	
   995	function evalDelegate(
   996	  run: Run,
   997	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "delegate" }>,
   998	  frame: Frame,
   999	  local?: LocalOk,
  1000	): NodeResult {
  1001	  if (decl.dependsOn?.some((tag) => local?.get(tag) === false) === true) return { ok: true };
  1002	  const owner = frame.parentValue;
  1003	  const by = isContainer(owner) ? containerGet(owner, decl.by) : undefined;
  1004	  if (typeof by !== "string") return { ok: true };
  1005	  const registration = run.registry(decl.registry)?.resolve(by) ?? null;
  1006	  if (registration === null) return { ok: true };
  1007	  if (registration.requiresRuntimeContext) run.runtimeContextBindings.push(parentPath(frame.path));
  1008	  const raw = frame.value === undefined ? undefined : materialize(frame.value, run.memo);
  1009	  const result = registration.validateAndNormalizeConfig(raw);
  1010	  if (result.ok) {
  1011	    run.effectiveConfigs.set(parentPath(frame.path), result.effective);
  1012	    return { ok: true, value: result.effective };
  1013	  }
  1014	  if (result.findings.length === 0) {
  1015	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.beltMessage, { ...baseSlots(frame), key: by, value: by }));
  1016	    return { ok: false };
  1017	  }
  1018	  for (const finding of result.findings) {
  1019	    const at = finding.path === "" ? frame.path : `${frame.path}.${finding.path}`;
  1020	    run.emit(at, finding.message, finding.code);
  1021	  }
  1022	  return { ok: false };
  1023	}

  1025	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1026	// Cross rules and the deferred queue.
  1027	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1028	
  1029	function unique(values: readonly string[]): string[] {
  1030	  return [...new Set(values)];
  1031	}
  1032	
  1033	/** vocabulary #12's `equals` — two-direction set equality, each direction
  1034	 * carrying its own path grain and wording (ch8-C16's measured form). */
  1035	function evaluateEquals(run: Run, frame: Frame, rule: EqualsRuleDecl): void {
  1036	  if (rule.dependsOn?.some((tag) => !run.reliable(tag)) === true) return;
  1037	  const left = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.left);
  1038	  const right = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.right);
  1039	  if (left.status !== "ok" || right.status !== "ok") return;
  1040	  const declared = unique(left.values);
  1041	  const used = unique(right.values);
  1042	  for (const value of used) {
  1043	    if (declared.includes(value)) continue;
  1044	    const at = render(rule.missingFromLeft.at, { value });
  1045	    run.emit(at, render(rule.missingFromLeft.message, { path: at, value }));

codex
Három futtatott reprodukció már megerősített, önálló hibát ad: (1) egy elgépelt, de nyelvtanilag szabályos selector átmegy és a membership rule nem fut; (2) az elgépelt `delegate.by` átmegy és a catalogot sem hívja; (3) value-class belsejében a lógó `dependsOn` kimarad a dependency-bejárásból. A normalizer esetén külön hiányzik mind a path-nyelvtan teljes ellenőrzése, mind a dinamikus mezőnevek feloldása; ezeket külön findingként kezelem.

 /**
  * The ENGINE's own suite (ADR-019's P3 build). It tests the VOCABULARY's
  * semantics over SYNTHETIC declarations — never the template
  * declaration, whose correctness is measured by the 1830-case corpus the
  * parity gate replayed.
  *
  * SENSITIVITY DISCIPLINE (process-log 2026-08-04, the non-discriminating
  * fixture and the vacuous mutant): every guard below carries BOTH its
  * fixture and a MUTANT declaration with that guard removed, and the
  * fixture must stop producing the finding against the mutant. A fixture
  * that fires either way proves nothing.
  *
  * Declaration-as-data makes the mutant cheap and exact: it is the same
  * object with one attribute deleted, not a hand-edited copy of an
  * engine. That is a property of the direction, and it is why this suite
  * can afford a mutant per guard.
  *
  * The register is ONE level: a per-claim fixture, the name list, and a
  * pinned count. No register tower (the documented anti-precedent).

+describe("the declaration GATE: a declaration that is not closed never becomes a surface", () => {
+  // A minimal CLOSED surface. Every guard fixture below is this surface
+  // with exactly ONE reference broken, and each pair proves discrimination:
+  // the broken form throws with its own problem, the corrected form does
+  // not throw at all.
+  const closed = (over: Partial<SurfaceDecl> = {}, rootOver: Record<string, unknown> = {}): SurfaceDecl =>
+    ({
+      substrate: templateFormat.substrate,
+      valueClasses: {
+        idClass: { kind: "string", tag: "vc", rows: ["x"], grammar: { re: "^[a-z]+$", message: "bad" } },
+      },
+      crossRules: [],
+      normalizers: [],
+      root: {
+        kind: "map.fixed",
+        tag: "root",
+        rows: ["x"],
+        containerMessage: "c",
+        unknownMessage: "u",
+        fields: { id: { kind: "valueClass", tag: "id", rows: ["x"], valueClass: "idClass" } },
+        ...rootOver,
+      },
+      ...over,
+    });
+
+  interface Guard {
+    readonly claim: string;
+    /** The surface with ONE reference broken. */
+    readonly broken: SurfaceDecl;
+    /** The problem the gate must name. */
+    readonly problem: RegExp;
+  }
+
+  const node = (extra: Record<string, unknown>): SurfaceDecl =>
+    closed({}, { fields: { id: { kind: "valueClass", tag: "id", rows: ["x"], valueClass: "idClass", ...extra } } });
+

+        names: { kind: "map.open", tag: "n", rows: ["x"], containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container", entry: { kind: "string", tag: "ne", rows: ["x"], typeMessage: "t" } },

+      },
+    });
+
+  const GUARDS: readonly Guard[] = [
+    {
+      claim: "an unknown value-class name — the measured shame case",
+      broken: closed({}, { fields: { id: { kind: "valueClass", tag: "id", rows: ["x"], valueClass: "TYPO" } } }),
+      problem: /value class "TYPO" is not declared/u,

+      problem: /dependsOn names "no-such-tag", which is not a declared tag/u,

+    {
+      claim: "a tag declared twice",
+      broken: node({ tag: "root" }),
+      problem: /tag "root" is declared more than once/u,
+    },
+    {
+      claim: "a node citing no ratified row",
+      broken: node({ rows: [] }),
+      problem: /cites no ratified row/u,
+    },
+    {
+      claim: "an issue code outside the closed namespace",
+      broken: closed({}, {
+        fields: { id: { kind: "enum", tag: "id", rows: ["x"], members: [{ value: "a" }], message: "m", code: "made_up_code" } },
+      }),
+      problem: /issue code "made_up_code" is outside the declared namespace/u,
+    },
+    {
+      claim: "a message using a placeholder the engine does not supply",
+      broken: closed({}, { unknownMessage: "unknown {notASlot}" }),

+    },
+    {
+      claim: "a normalizer operand path that is not document-rooted",
+      broken: closed({
+        normalizers: [{ tag: "n", rows: ["x"], hook: "expandAdvancesRound", over: "steps", edges: "t", advanceSet: "$.r", into: "f" }],

+    },

+      broken: closed({
+        valueClasses: { idClass: { kind: "string", tag: "vc", rows: ["x"], grammar: { re: "^[a-z", message: "bad" } } },
+      }),
+      problem: /is not a valid regular expression/u,
+    },
+  ];
+
+  for (const guard of GUARDS) {
+    it(`${guard.claim} — the gate REFUSES it`, () => {
+      expect(() => defineSurface(guard.broken)).toThrow(SurfaceDeclarationError);
+      expect(closureProblems(guard.broken).join("\n")).toMatch(guard.problem);
+    });
+  }
+
+  it("DISCRIMINATION: the same surface with every reference resolved passes the gate", () => {
+    // Every fixture above is `closed()` with ONE thing broken. If the gate
+    // fired unconditionally, this would throw too — which is what makes the
+    // refusals above evidence rather than noise.
+    expect(closureProblems(closed())).toStrictEqual([]);
+    expect(() => defineSurface(closed())).not.toThrow();
+    expect(closureProblems(withSelector("$.names"))).toStrictEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it("the guard register is complete, unique and PINNED", () => {
+    expect(GUARDS).toHaveLength(13);
+    expect(new Set(GUARDS.map((guard) => guard.claim)).size).toBe(GUARDS.length);
+  });
+
+  it("the LIVE declaration is closed — no unresolved reference reaches a document", () => {
+    expect(closureProblems(templateFormat)).toStrictEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it("the LIVE declaration is frozen at EVERY level, not only the root (ADR-019 D4)", () => {
+    const root = templateFormat.root as unknown as { readonly fields: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>> };
+    const nested = root.fields["start"]?.["memberOf"] as { message: string };
+    expect(Object.isFrozen(templateFormat)).toBe(true);
+    expect(Object.isFrozen(root)).toBe(true);
+    expect(Object.isFrozen(nested)).toBe(true);
+    expect(() => {
+      nested.message = "MUTATED";
+    }).toThrow(TypeError);
+  });
+
+  it("the `..` form the vocabulary once documented is REFUSED, not silently inert", () => {
+    // The regression this gate exists for: before it, a declaration written
+    // to the documented form compiled, ran, and validated nothing.
+    const problems = closureProblems(withSelector("..names"));
+    expect(problems).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(runSurface(withSelector("$.names"), { names: { a: "1" }, pick: "zz" }, { channel: { kind: "direct" } }).findings)
+      .toHaveLength(1);
   });
 });

 /**
  * ADR-019 D1: THE ENGINE — one validator over declaration DATA, running
  * the SAME declaration on the file-walk and direct-construction channels.
  *
  * The engine consumes a `SurfaceDecl`; it knows the VOCABULARY and knows
  * nothing about any particular rule. A per-rule branch here (a literal
  * `if (rule === X)`) is ADR-019 D9 tripwire #2 — a STOP and a scope
  * review, never a work-around. Where two measured lanes disagree on a
  * grain (path address, lane order, gating), the disagreement is DECLARED
  * (`at:`, `laneOrder:`, `gating:`), which is the same instrument the
  * audit's §5 F3 identified for path grain.
  *
  * Channel symmetry is structural, not argued (D1): attributes that read
  * SOURCE text (`sourceForm`, the deep key-stringness scan) are declared
  * once with `channel: "file"`; the engine runs them where a source exists
  * and skips them where none does.
  *
  * This module VALIDATES and applies plain `default:` materialization. It
  * does NOT derive values — derivation is the separately-named normalizer
  * (D3, `normalizer.ts`).
  */
 
+/** The engine's INTERNAL address for a declaration position
+ * (`$.steps.*.role`). It is NOT one of the declaration's three path
+ * languages (design review F11) — no declaration ever writes one; the walk
+ * builds them to key its reliability and completion bookkeeping. */
+type DeclPath = string;
+
 /** The channel the declaration is being run on. */
 export type EngineChannel =
   | { readonly kind: "file"; readonly doc: Document; readonly source: string }
   | { readonly kind: "direct" };
 
 export interface EngineOptions {
   readonly channel: EngineChannel;
   readonly catalog?: GateCatalog;
 }
 
 export interface EngineRun {
   readonly findings: readonly ValidationFinding[];
   /** The value with plain defaults materialized and file-channel maps
    * realized as own-property records. Undefined when the root container
    * lane failed (no operand exists). */
   readonly normalized: unknown;
   /** Binding path → the registration's EFFECTIVE config, collected as the
    * `delegate` lanes resolve. The normalizer's `materializeEffectiveConfigs`
    * hook consumes it. */
   readonly effectiveConfigs: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>;
   /** Every binding whose resolved registration declares
    * `requiresRuntimeContext` — the operand of the R3 residual cross-rule,
    * which stays hand code (audit §4). */
   readonly runtimeContextBindings: readonly string[];
 }

   // The REFERENCING site's gating decides, not the shared class's.
   if (!result.ok && decl.gating === true) run.mark(frame.decl, false);
   return result;
 }

   const emitMissing = (name: string): void => {

 function childFrame(parent: Frame, key: string, value: unknown, parentValue: unknown): Frame {
   return {
     decl: `${parent.decl}.${key}`,
     path: joinPath(parent.path, key),
     value,
     parentValue,
     ownerKey: parent.ownerKey,
